for the petitioners had informed the Court that the issue raised in

and batch and by following the aforesaid common judgment, all
these Appeals are also liable to be disposed of.

considered view that all these Appeals also deserve to be disposed
of by following the aforesaid common judgment, dated
29.04.2025, passed by this Court in L.A.A.S.No.57 of 2024 and
batch.

aforesaid common judgment, dated 29.04.2025, passed by this
Court in L.A.A.S.No.57 of 2024 and batch, and the market value

Appeal under Section 54 of Land Acquisition Act aggrieved by the order and
decree dated 1 1-03-20-10 passed in o.P.No 74 of 2009 on the file of the cou( of the
Senior Civil Judge at Gadwal.
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the judgment and Decree of the Lower Court and the material papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri S. Venkateshwar Reddy,
Advocate for the Appellants and GP FOR APPEALS for the Respondent.
This Court doth Order and Decree as follows:
1. That the appeal be and hereby is disposed of in terms of the judgment dated
29-04-2025 rendered in L A A.S.No.57 of 2OZ4 & batch.
2. The the Market value is enhanced by two more times (1+2) than the value
fixed by the Land Acquisition Officer;
3. That the appellants shall be entitled to all statutory benefits as stipulated
under the amended Land Acquisition Act,

To,
kam
w
4. That the claimant be and hereby is not entitled to claim interest for the delay
period of 5402 days in preferring the appeal as specifically ordered by this
Court while condoning the delay;and
5. That there shall be no order as to costs in this appeal,
SD/- K. SRINIVASA RAO

Appeal under Section 54 of Land Acquisition Act aggrieved by the order and
decree dated 06-07-2006 passed in O.P.No 57 of 2005 on the file of the Court of the
Senior Crvil Judge at Gadwal.
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the ludgment and Decree of the Lower Court and the material papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri V I\,4anohar Rao, Advocate for the
Appellants and GP FOR APPEALS for the Respondent
This Court doth Order and Decree as follows
'1 . That the appeal be and hereby is disposed of in terms of the judgment dated
29-04-2025 rendered in L.A.A S No 57 of 2024 & batch:
2. The the Market value is enhanced by two more times (1+2) than the value
fixed by the Land Acquisition Officer,
3 That the appellants shall be entitled to all statutory benefits as stipulated
under the amended Land Acquisition Act;
4. Thal the claimant be and hereby is not entitled to claim interest for the delay
period of 7100 days in preferring the appeal as specifically ordered by this
Court while condoning the delay, and
5. That there shall be no order as to costs in this appeal.
SD/- K. SRINIVASA RAO

Vide order dated 16.08.2004 in I.A No. 189 of2004 on the file of the learned Senior
Civil Judge's Court, Gadwal.
'I

Appeal under Section 54 of Land Acquisition Act aggrieved by the order and
decree dated 14-01-2005 passed in O.P.No.34 of 2003 on the frle of the Court of the
Senior Civil Judge at Gadwal.
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the JUdgnrent and Decree of the Lower Court and the material papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri V. Manohar Rao, Advocate for the
Appellants and GP FOR APPEALS for the Respondent.
This Court doth Order and Decree as follows:
1. That the appeal be and hereby is disposed of in terms of the judgment dated
29 04-2025 rendered in L.A A.S No.57 ot 2024 & batch;
2. The the l\ilarket value is enhanced by two more times (l+2) than the value
fixed by the Land Acquisition Officer;
3. That the appellants shall be entitled to all statutory benefits as stipulated
under the amended Land Acquisition Act;
4. That the claimant be and hereby is not entitled to claim interest for the delay
period of 6990 days in preferring the appeal as specifically ordered by this
Court while condoning the delay; and
5. That there shall be no order as to costs in this appeal.
SD/- K. SRINIVASA RAO

-v
/
Appeal under Section 54 of Land Acquisrtion Act aggrieved by the order and
decree dated 03-11-2008 passed in o.p.No 40 of 2oo7 on the file of the court of the
Senior Civil Judge at Gadwal,
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the judgment and Decree of the Lower court and the materiar papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri S. Venkateshwar Reddy,
Advocate for the Appellants and Gp FOR AppEALS for the Respondent.
This Court doth Order and Decree as follows:
1. That the appeal be and hereby is disposed of in terms of the judgment dated
29-04-2025 rendered in L.A,A.S,No 57 of 2024 & batch;
2. The the Market varue is enhanced by two more times (1+2) than the varue
fixed by the Land Acquisition Officer;
3. That the appellants shal be entitled to al statutory benefits as stipurated
under the amended Land Acquisition Act;
4 That the claimant be and hereby is not entitred to craim interest for the deray
period of 2951 days in preferring the appear as specificaly ordered by this
Court while condoning the delay; and
5. That there shall be no order as to costs in this appeal.
SD/- K. SRINIVASA RAO
JOINT REGISTRAR

Appeal under Section 54 of Land Acquisition Act aggrieved by the order and
decree dated 17-08-2009 passed rn O.P.No.36 of 2007 on the file of the Court of the
Senror Civil Judge at Gadwal.
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the judgment and Decree of the Lower Court and the material papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri V. IVlanohar Rao, Advocate for the
Appellants and GP FOR APPEALS for the Respondent.
This Court doth Order and Decree as follows:
.1. That the appeal be and hereby is disposed of in terms of the 1-udgment dated
29-04-2025 rendered in L.A.,{.S.No.57 of 2O24 & batch;
2. The the fiilarket value is enhanced by two more times (1+2) than the value
fixed by the Land Acquisition Officer;
3. That the appellants shall be entitled to all statutory benefits as stipulated
under the amended Land Acquisition Act;
4. That the clarmant be and hereby is not entitled to claim interest for the delay
period of 5147 days in preferring the appeal as specifically ordered by this
Court while condoning the delay; and
5. That there shall be no order as to costs in this appeal.
SD/. K. SRINIVASA RAO
